# FeralPig Document Ingestion Index

Version: v0.1  
Date: 2026-09-29

## Objective and scope

Account for every physical project file and every recursively indexed archive/Office package member, preserve byte identity, and attach source-derived operating instructions. This is an ingestion and continuation record for a U.S. working-draft patent matter and separate staged software; it is not a legal opinion, current-law verification, or filing certification.

## Source record and coverage

The pre-implementation read-only inventory contained 446 physical files and 126,166,907 bytes. Final checkpoint: **452 physical files**, **1509 virtual members**, **1961 indexed records**, and **389 files selected for publication**; 63 physical files are retained locally but excluded (Git internals/Python caches/local project configuration). Index/manifest self records are listed separately below to avoid circular hashes.

All unique documents were extracted without page limits: 53 unique PDF contents / 4323 pages, including all 43 MPEP members / 4216 pages. DOCX extraction includes package XML, tables, headers, footers, comments and metadata; workbooks include every sheet/cell, formulas/caches and comments. Raw and decoded content was credential-scanned. Four pattern findings were audited as synthetic test fixtures (three test_presets.py contexts and one excluded bytecode copy); no actual credential was identified. This is a scan result, not a guarantee.

Extraction and substantive reading are separate. Canonical patent documents and current tracker received substantive review; all twelve current and historical figure pages received visual review. Historical artifacts were reviewed for chronology/provenance, not re-certified. The MPEP pack received complete extraction and structure indexing plus focused operative passage review; **an every-word legal-manual review has not been performed**. Package XML/binary assets are interpreted through their parent sources. No OCR, native spreadsheet recalculation, current-law confirmation, live API/EndNote/browser integration, or website/DNS deployment is claimed.

Exact duplicates share extracted content but retain a separate path entry and inherited source authority. All hashes below are SHA-256 of exact original bytes, not extracted text. A local hash is not live OneDrive read-back. Source rules are analyst-derived operating instructions, not purported quotations or legal conclusions.

## Current controls and unresolved evidence

- [Master instructions](FeralPig_ProjectInstructions_v0.2_20260929.md) and [resume plan](FeralPig_ResumePlan_v0.1_20260929.md) provide source pinpoints for R01-R17. The current patent tracker/specification/claims/audit/figures remain v1.3/v0.7/v0.7/v0.5/v0.2.
- Retained status/master/roadmap/index summaries are stale; two index hashes disagree with manifest v1.3 and convenience copies. The new manifest records local reality without asserting online sync reconciliation.
- Out-of-range support citations, citation drift, the shifted disclosure row, family-table omissions, antecedent concerns, figure-label defects and preliminary prior-art pinpoints remain open. No patent content or workbook cell was repaired in this checkpoint.
- The MPEP ZIP is Ninth Edition Revision01.2024, published November2024. Laws/rules editor notes state January31,2024 cutoffs. Later navigation notes do not update those bytes. AppendixAI means PCT Administrative Instructions.
- The Reference Suite and LayneIP/Cloudflare imports are separate staged workstreams with unverified live integrations and incomplete site packaging.
- The scoped user instruction authorizes public RobertMLayne/feral-pig-startup publication of all selected records/source, including invention/inventor material and historical logs. It does not establish attorney clearance, filing readiness or authority for other external actions.

## Corresponding instruction registry

| Rule | Operating instruction |
|---|---|
| A01 | Specification: use the current numbered disclosure; distinguish inventor-confirmed facts, public examples and AI formalizations; retain open enabling-support gates and WORKING DRAFT status. |
| A02 | Claims: use tracker-controlled numbering, parentage and risks; preserve the unrestricted universe; verify antecedent basis and current support; do not adopt historical family tables or claim ceilings. |
| A03 | Tracker: use current claim/task records, but reconcile documented stale cells, shifted fields and support references before reliance. Historical workbooks and the template are noncontrolling examples. |
| A04 | Support/figure audit: remap actual claim limitations to current numbered disclosure and visual figures; old QA assertions and paragraph citations require current verification. |
| A05 | Inventor questionnaire: obtain non-leading human answers, contributor/date evidence and confirm/correct/reject dispositions; unanswered questions remain open. |
| A06 | Status/master/roadmap: preserve milestone and business sequence as context; current tracker overrides stale counts, versions and resume tasks. |
| A07 | Manifest/index: treat versioned manifests as dated checkpoints; retain actual local hashes and discrepancies; do not infer live OneDrive verification or promote stale index links. |
| A08 | Instructions: the current master and root AGENTS entry point control project workflow. Earlier instructions are retained predecessors; external actions require the current scoped user authorization. |
| A09 | MPEP/legal source: retain edition and section/page pinpoints; use the relevant chapter as reference, distinguish law/rules/guidance, and verify current primary authority before legal reliance. This ZIP is Rev.01.2024, published November2024; laws/rules cutoff January31,2024. |
| A10 | Research notes/technical sources: retain citation provenance, source dates and limitations; public examples are not inventor conception, efficacy evidence or regulatory permission. |
| A11 | Conversation/export/transcript: retain original context and provenance; treat generated reasoning and entertainment material as leads, not current drafting authority or proven legal/technical facts. |
| A12 | Reference Suite: preserve job/record/run contracts, immutable raw bytes, provenance, source facts versus review corrections, explicit acquisition preview and exact-host/HTTPS boundaries; label live integrations unverified. |
| A13 | LayneIP: preserve dirty imported source and routes; author structured content/templates; reconcile missing packaging before build claims; keep drafts and public generation distinct. |
| A14 | Cloudflare/domain work: retained inventory is historical and blocked; obtain fresh authenticated inventory before separately authorized changes; preserve mail/unrelated records and recovery evidence. |
| A15 | Configuration: record paths and settings as facts; old Documents/Startup paths and automatic approvals are not authorization. Do not change security, network or deployment settings in this assignment. |
| A16 | Maintenance script: read as operational provenance; do not execute rename, move or destructive deduplication during this checkpoint. Preserve original records and naming. |
| A17 | Asset/package: preserve exact original bytes, media/format metadata and container relationships; an asset or package name cannot confer patent drafting authority. |
| A18 | Office package part: interpret XML, relationships, drawings, comments and metadata in the parent document context; inherit parent authority. A reusable template part alone is not evidence of invention conception. |
| A19 | Test record: preserve code/results and fixture context; historical fake-provider passes are not current live integration validation. Synthetic credential fixtures are not operational credentials. |
| A20 | Contact/editorial content: preserve documented professional facts and draft/public status; repository publication does not authorize contacting anyone or publishing the website. |
